04680: function empurrar(e, fx, fz, alt) {
04681:   try {
04682:     if (e.typeId === "minecraft:player") e.applyKnockback({ x: fx, z: fz }, alt);
04683:     else e.applyImpulse({ x: fx, y: alt, z: fz });
04684:   } catch (err) {}
04685: }

02682: function ensureRule(r){

02684:   if(!Array.isArray(r.condGroups) || !r.condGroups.length){

02689:   // O editor usa essa referência durante o toque; recriá-la no update() fazia
02690:   // o botão + condição apontar para um grupo antigo e não adicionar nada.
02691:   const first=r.condGroups[0];
02692:   if(r.condGroups.length>1){
02693:     const todas=[]; for(const g of r.condGroups) todas.push(...(g.conds||[]));

03049: function hl(src, json){
03050:   let s = src.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
03051:   const store=[]; const keep=(cls,txt)=>{store.push(`<span class="${cls}">${txt}</span>`);return `\u0000${store.length-1}\u0000`;};
03052:   s = s.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|\/\/.*$/gm, m=>keep('cm',m));
03053:   s = s.replace(/`(?:[^`\\]|\\.)*`|"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|'(?:[^'\\]|\\.)*'/g, m=>keep('s',m));
03054:   if (!json){
03055:     s = s.replace(/\b(import|from|const|let|return|if|for|of|new|try|catch|function|then|null|undefined|true|false)\b/g,'<span class="k">$1</span>');
03056:     s = s.replace(/\b([a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*)(?=\()/g,'<span class="fn">$1</span>');
03057:   } else {
03058:     s = s.replace(/\b(true|false|null)\b/g,'<span class="k">$1</span>');
03059:   }
03060:   s = s.replace(/\b(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\b/g,'<span class="n">$1</span>');
03061:   s = s.replace(/\u0000(\d+)\u0000/g,(_,i)=>store[+i]);
03062:   return s;
03063: }

00510: const cnum = (v,d) => { const n=Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n)?n:d; };
00511: const off = (v, d) => { const n = num(v, d); if (typeof n === 'string') return `+ ${n}`; return n < 0 ? `- ${Math.abs(n)}` : `+ ${n}`; };
00512: const uid = () => (crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID()
00513:   : 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, c => {
00514:       const r = Math.random()*16|0; return (c==='x'?r:(r&0x3|0x8)).toString(16); }));

00533: /* =====================================================================
00534:    DIREÇÃO RELATIVA — frente / atrás / esquerda / direita
00535:    Helpers abaixo são injetados no código gerado só quando usados.
00536:    ===================================================================== */
00537: const HELPERS = {
00538:   vetorRelativo:
00539: `/** Converte frente/lado na direção em que a entidade está olhando. */
00540: function vetorRelativo(ent, frente = 0, lado = 0) {
00541:   const v = ent.getViewDirection();
00542:   const h = Math.sqrt(v.x * v.x + v.z * v.z) || 1;
00543:   const fx = v.x / h, fz = v.z / h;
00544:   return { x: fx * frente - fz * lado, z: fz * frente + fx * lado };
00545: }`,
00546:   posRelativa:
00547: `/** Posição ao lado da entidade. frente(+)/atrás(-), direita(+)/esquerda(-). */
00548: function posRelativa(ent, frente = 0, lado = 0, altura = 0) {
00549:   const d = vetorRelativo(ent, frente, lado);
00550:   const l = ent.location;
00551:   return { x: l.x + d.x, y: l.y + altura, z: l.z + d.z };
00552: }`,
00553:   empurrar:
00554: `/** Empurra na direção que a entidade olha. Jogador e mob usam APIs diferentes. */
00555: function empurrar(ent, frente = 0, lado = 0, vertical = 0) {
00556:   const d = vetorRelativo(ent, frente, lado);
00557:   if (ent.typeId === "minecraft:player") ent.applyKnockback(d, vertical);
00558:   else ent.applyImpulse({ x: d.x, y: vertical, z: d.z });
00559: }`,

00564:   const item = eq?.getEquipment(slot);

02987: function groupedOptions(reg, sel){
02988:   const groups = {};
02989:   Object.entries(reg).forEach(([k,v])=>{ (groups[v.group||'Geral'] ||= []).push([k,v.label]); });
02990:   return Object.entries(groups).map(([g,items])=>
02991:     `<optgroup label="${g}">` + items.map(([k,l])=>
02992:       `<option value="${k}" ${k===sel?'selected':''}>${l}</option>`).join('') + `</optgroup>`
02993:   ).join('');
02994: }
02995: 
02996: function renderConditions(groups){
02997:   if(!Array.isArray(groups)||!groups.length) groups.push({op:'and',not:false,conds:[]});
02998:   const g=ensureGroup(groups[0]); g.op='and'; g.not=false;
02999:   const gid=uiReg(g,UI_GROUPS);
03000:   let h=`<div class="condition-list">`;
03001:   if(!g.conds.length) h+=`<div class="condition-empty">Sem condição: esta regra executa sempre que o QUANDO acontecer.</div>`;
03002:   g.conds.forEach((c,ci)=>{
03003:     if(!c.id)c.id=uid();
03004:     const C=CONDITIONS[c.type]||CONDITIONS.hasTag;
03005:     h+=`<div class="card"><div class="card-top"><select class="inp" data-cond-id="${c.id}" data-cond-gid="${gid}" data-cond-index="${ci}" data-picker-title="Escolher condição" onchange="setCondUI('${gid}',${ci},this.value)">${groupedOptions(CONDITIONS,c.type)}</select><button class="icobtn" onclick="delCondUI('${gid}',${ci})">✕</button></div>${C.fields.length?`<div class="params">${C.fields.map(f=>field(f,c.p?.[f.k],`((v)=>{UI_GROUPS['${gid}'].conds[${ci}].p['${f.k}']=v;update()})`)).join('')}</div>`:''}</div>`;
03006:   });
03007:   h+=`<button class="addbtn" onclick="addCondUI('${gid}')">+ condição</button></div>`;
03008:   return h;
03009: }
03010: function renderActList(list,ri,tg,T,depth=0,scope=false){
03011:   const lid=uiReg(list), nested=depth? ' nested-actions':''; let h=`<div class="${nested.trim()}">`;
03012:   list.forEach((a,ai)=>{ensureAction(a);const A=ACTIONS[a.type]||ACTIONS.msg;const isFlow=!!A.flow;h+=`<div class="${isFlow?'flow-card':'card'}"><div class="card-top"><select class="inp" onchange="setActUI('${lid}',${ai},this.value)">${groupedOptions(ACTIONS,a.type)}</select><button class="icobtn" onclick="delActUI('${lid}',${ai})">✕</button></div>`;
03013:     if(A.flow==='repeat'||A.flow==='foreach'){
03014:       h+=`<div class="params">${A.fields.map(f=>field(f,a.p[f.k],`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p['${f.k}']=v;update()})`)).join('')}</div><div class="flow-children"><div class="flow-head"><b>${A.flow==='repeat'?'ações repetidas':'ações para cada entidade'}</b></div>${renderActList(a.children,ri,tg,T,depth+1,A.flow==='foreach'||scope)}</div>`;
03015:     } else if(A.flow==='random'){
03016:       h+=`<div class="hint">Uma opção é escolhida de acordo com o peso. Peso 10 é dez vezes mais provável que peso 1.</div>`; a.choices.forEach((ch,ci)=>{const clid=uiReg(a.choices);h+=`<div class="choice"><div class="choice-head"><div><label class="f">Nome</label><input class="inp" value="${String(ch.label||'Opção').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" oninput="UI_LISTS['${clid}'][${ci}].label=this.value;update()"></div><div><label class="f">Peso</label><input type="number" class="inp" value="${ch.weight??1}" oninput="UI_LISTS['${clid}'][${ci}].weight=this.value;update()"></div><button class="icobtn" onclick="UI_LISTS['${clid}'].splice(${ci},1);render()">✕</button></div><div class="flow-children">${renderActList(ch.acts,ri,tg,T,depth+1,scope)}</div></div>`}); h+=`<button class="addbtn" onclick="UI_LISTS['${uiReg(a.choices)}'].push({label:'Nova opção',weight:1,acts:[]});render()">+ opção aleatória</button>`;
03017:     } else {
03018:       h+=`<div class="params">`;
03019:       if(['player','entity','loc'].includes(A.needs)){
03020:         h+=`<div><label class="f">Alvo</label><select class="inp" onchange="if(this.value==='scope'){UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].scopeTarget=true}else{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].scopeTarget=false;UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].tgt=+this.value}render()">${scope?`<option value="scope" ${a.scopeTarget?'selected':''}>entidade do PARA CADA</option>`:''}${tg.map((t,i)=>`<option value="${i}" ${!a.scopeTarget&&a.tgt===i?'selected':''}>${t.label}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`;
03021:         const chosen=a.scopeTarget&&scope?null:tg[a.tgt]; if(chosen?.isNear){h+=field({k:'raio',t:'num',label:'Raio (blocos)',def:10},a.p.raio,`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p.raio=v;update()})`)+field({k:'tipo',t:'text',label:'Só este tipo',ph:'minecraft:zombie'},a.p.tipo,`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p.tipo=v;render()})`)+field({k:'familia',t:'text',label:'Só esta família',ph:'monster'},a.p.familia,`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p.familia=v;update()})`);}
03022:       }
03023:       if(A.needs==='loc'){
03024:         const opts=[['target','no alvo']];if(T.hasBlock)opts.push(['block','no bloco']);opts.push(['rotated','relativo ao alvo'],['coords','coordenadas fixas']);h+=`<div><label class="f">Onde</label><select class="inp" onchange="UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].loc=this.value;render()">${opts.map(o=>`<option value="${o[0]}" ${a.loc===o[0]?'selected':''}>${o[1]}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`;
03025:         if(a.loc==='coords')[['lx','X',0],['ly','Y',80],['lz','Z',0]].forEach(([k,l,d])=>h+=field({k,t:'num',label:l,def:d},a.p[k],`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p['${k}']=v;update()})`));
03026:         if(a.loc==='rotated'){h+=field({k:'dir',t:'select',label:'Direção',opts:DIRS,def:'frente'},a.p.dir,`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p.dir=v;update()})`)+field({k:'dist',t:'num',label:'Distância',def:3},a.p.dist,`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p.dist=v;update()})`)+field({k:'offsetY',t:'num',label:'Altura',def:0},a.p.offsetY,`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p.offsetY=v;update()})`);}
03027:       }
03028:       h+=(A.fields||[]).map(f=>field(f,a.p[f.k],`((v)=>{UI_LISTS['${lid}'][${ai}].p['${f.k}']=v;update()})`)).join(''); h+=`</div>`;
03029:     }
03030:     h+=`</div>`;
03031:   });
03032:   h+=`<button class="addbtn" onclick="addActUI('${lid}','msg',${scope?'true':'false'})">+ ação</button></div>`;return h;
03033: }
03034: function render(){
03035:   UI_LISTS=Object.create(null);UI_GROUPS=Object.create(null);uiSeq=0;rules.forEach(ensureRule);const box=document.getElementById('rules');

03045: function setTrig(ri,v){rules[ri].trig=v;rules[ri].tp={};rules[ri].subj=0;rules[ri].acts.forEach(a=>{a.tgt=0;a.loc='target'});render();}
03046: function setCond(ri,ci,v){ensureRule(rules[ri]);rules[ri].condGroups[0].conds[ci]={type:v,p:{}};render();}
03047: function setAct(ri,ai,v){ensureRule(rules[ri]);const old=rules[ri].acts[ai];rules[ri].acts[ai]=newAction(v,!!old?.scopeTarget);render();}

03049: function hl(src, json){
03050:   let s = src.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
03051:   const store=[]; const keep=(cls,txt)=>{store.push(`<span class="${cls}">${txt}</span>`);return `\u0000${store.length-1}\u0000`;};
03052:   s = s.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|\/\/.*$/gm, m=>keep('cm',m));
03053:   s = s.replace(/`(?:[^`\\]|\\.)*`|"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|'(?:[^'\\]|\\.)*'/g, m=>keep('s',m));
03054:   if (!json){
03055:     s = s.replace(/\b(import|from|const|let|return|if|for|of|new|try|catch|function|then|null|undefined|true|false)\b/g,'<span class="k">$1</span>');
03056:     s = s.replace(/\b([a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*)(?=\()/g,'<span class="fn">$1</span>');
03057:   } else {
03058:     s = s.replace(/\b(true|false|null)\b/g,'<span class="k">$1</span>');
03059:   }
03060:   s = s.replace(/\b(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\b/g,'<span class="n">$1</span>');
03061:   s = s.replace(/\u0000(\d+)\u0000/g,(_,i)=>store[+i]);
03062:   return s;
03063: }

04565: /* =====================================================================
04566:    FORJA IN-GAME
04567:    Monte regras dentro do jogo. Mesma lógica da Forja de Scripts do site.
04568:    Abrir o menu: !forja no chat, /scriptevent forja:menu,
04569:    ou usar o item de configuração agachado.
04570:    ===================================================================== */
04571: 
04572: const CFG = {
04573:   item: __ITEM__,          // item que abre o menu quando usado agachado
04574:   chat: __CHAT__,          // palavra no chat que abre o menu
04575:   soAdmin: __ADMIN__,      // se true, só quem tem a tag abaixo abre o menu
04576:   tagAdmin: "forja_admin"
04577: };
04578: 
04579: /* ---------- armazenamento em pedaços (driblando o limite por propriedade) ---------- */
04580: const CHUNK = 1800;
04581: function salvar(regras) {
04582:   const txt = JSON.stringify(regras);
04583:   const n = Math.ceil(txt.length / CHUNK) || 1;
04584:   world.setDynamicProperty("forja:n", n);
04585:   for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) world.setDynamicProperty("forja:d" + i, txt.slice(i * CHUNK, (i + 1) * CHUNK));
04586:   for (let i = n; i < n + 24; i++) {
04587:     if (world.getDynamicProperty("forja:d" + i) === undefined) break;
04588:     world.setDynamicProperty("forja:d" + i, undefined);
04589:   }
04590: }
04591: function carregar() {
04592:   const n = world.getDynamicProperty("forja:n");
04593:   if (!n) return [];
04594:   let txt = "";
04595:   for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) txt += world.getDynamicProperty("forja:d" + i) ?? "";
04596:   try { const r = JSON.parse(txt); return Array.isArray(r) ? r : []; } catch (e) { return []; }
04597: }
04598: let REGRAS = [];
04599: 
04600: /* ---------- utilidades ---------- */
04601: const ok = (e) => { try { return typeof e?.isValid === "function" ? e.isValid() : !!e?.isValid; } catch (err) { return false; } };
04602: let __evalCtx = {}, __evalSuj = undefined, __evalLocals = Object.create(null);
04603: function calcExpr(src) {
04604:   let i = 0; src = String(src ?? "");
04605:   const ws = () => { while (/\s/.test(src[i] || "")) i++; };
04606:   function fator() {
04607:     ws();
04608:     if (src[i] === "+") { i++; return fator(); }
04609:     if (src[i] === "-") { i++; return -fator(); }
04610:     if (src[i] === "(") { i++; const v = soma(); ws(); if (src[i] !== ")") throw new Error("parenteses"); i++; return v; }
04611:     const m = src.slice(i).match(/^(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)(?:e[+-]?\d+)?/i);
04612:     if (!m) throw new Error("numero"); i += m[0].length; return Number(m[0]);
05358:     }
05359: 
05360:     if (r.on === "vidaCruzou") {
05361:       if (TICK % 10 >= 2) continue;
05362:       let set = EST.vida.get(r.id); if (!set) { set = new Set(); EST.vida.set(r.id, set); }
05363:       for (const pl of jogadores) {
05364:         if (vidaPct(pl) >= nn(p.pct, 30)) { set.delete(pl.id); continue; }
05365:         if (set.has(pl.id)) continue;
05366:         set.add(pl.id);
05367:         rodar(r, { player: pl, ent: pl });
05368:       }
05369:       continue;
05370:     }
05371:   }
05372: }, 2);
05373: 
05374: /* ---------- item que abre o menu ---------- */
05375: sub(world.afterEvents, "itemUse", (ev) => {
05376:   if (!CFG.item) return;
05377:   if (ev.itemStack?.typeId !== CFG.item) return;
05378:   if (!ev.source.isSneaking) return;
05379:   const pl = ev.source;
05380:   system.run(() => abrirMenu(pl));
05381: });

05408: function normIgAction(a) {
05409:   if (!a || typeof a !== "object") return;
05410:   if (!a.p) a.p={};
05411:   if (a.tgt===undefined) a.tgt="auto";
05412:   if (!a.loc) a.loc="target";

05414:   a.adv={chance:100,repeat:1,interval:0,onFail:"continue",enabled:true};
05415:   if (a.t==="__repeat"||a.t==="__foreach") { if(!Array.isArray(a.children))a.children=[]; for(const x of a.children)normIgAction(x); }
05416:   if (a.t==="__random") { if(!Array.isArray(a.choices)||!a.choices.length)a.choices=[{label:"Opção A",weight:1,acts:[]},{label:"Opção B",weight:1,acts:[]}]; for(const c of a.choices){if(!Array.isArray(c.acts))c.acts=[];for(const x of c.acts)normIgAction(x);} }
05417: }

05885:   function collectOptions(sel){
05886:     const out=[];
05887:     for(const node of Array.from(sel.children)){
05888:       if(node.tagName==='OPTGROUP'){
05889:         out.push({kind:'group',label:node.label||''});
05890:         for(const op of Array.from(node.children)){ if(op.hidden) continue; out.push({kind:'option',group:node.label||'',value:op.value,text:(op.textContent||'').trim(),disabled:op.disabled,selected:op.selected}); }
05891:       }else if(node.tagName==='OPTION'){
05892:         if(!node.hidden) out.push({kind:'option',group:'',value:node.value,text:(node.textContent||'').trim(),disabled:node.disabled,selected:node.selected});
05893:       }
05894:     }
05895:     return out;
05896:   }

05908:       }

05910:     }

03045: function setTrig(ri,v){rules[ri].trig=v;rules[ri].tp={};rules[ri].subj=0;rules[ri].acts.forEach(a=>{a.tgt=0;a.loc='target'});render();}

03048: 
03049: function hl(src, json){
03050:   let s = src.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
03051:   const store=[]; const keep=(cls,txt)=>{store.push(`<span class="${cls}">${txt}</span>`);return `\u0000${store.length-1}\u0000`;};
03052:   s = s.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|\/\/.*$/gm, m=>keep('cm',m));
03053:   s = s.replace(/`(?:[^`\\]|\\.)*`|"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|'(?:[^'\\]|\\.)*'/g, m=>keep('s',m));
03054:   if (!json){
03055:     s = s.replace(/\b(import|from|const|let|return|if|for|of|new|try|catch|function|then|null|undefined|true|false)\b/g,'<span class="k">$1</span>');
03056:     s = s.replace(/\b([a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*)(?=\()/g,'<span class="fn">$1</span>');
03057:   } else {
03058:     s = s.replace(/\b(true|false|null)\b/g,'<span class="k">$1</span>');
03059:   }
03060:   s = s.replace(/\b(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\b/g,'<span class="n">$1</span>');
03061:   s = s.replace(/\u0000(\d+)\u0000/g,(_,i)=>store[+i]);
03062:   return s;
03063: }

03066: function update(){
03067:   const g = generateJS();
03068:   currentJS = g.code;
03069:   currentMF = generateManifest(g.usesUI);
03070:   const nm = (document.getElementById('aName').value||'meu-addon')
03071:     .toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'') || 'meu-addon';
03072:   const v = versions();
03073:   const tree =
03074: `USANDO NO ADDONS MAKER (MAM)
03075: ────────────────────────────
03076: 1. Toque em "Copiar main.js" aqui embaixo.
03077: 2. No MAM, abra seu addon e vá na parte de
03078:    scripts. Crie um script novo e cole o código.
03083:      @minecraft/server-ui   ${v.ui}
03084:      min_engine_version     ${v.engine.join('.')}
03085: 
03086:    (é exatamente o bloco "dependencies" da
03087:     aba manifest.json — se o MAM deixar editar
03088:     o manifest, use "Copiar manifest".)
03089: 
03090: 5. Exporte o addon e teste no mundo.
03091: 
03092: Se o script não rodar no MAM, quase sempre é
03093: a versão do @minecraft/server diferente da que
03094: o app coloca no manifest. Confira esse número
03095: primeiro.
03096: 
03097: USANDO O .MCADDON PRONTO
03098: ────────────────────────
03099: ${nm}_BP/
03100: ├── manifest.json
03101: └── scripts/
03102:     └── main.js
03103: 
03104: 1. Baixe o .mcaddon e abra com o Minecraft.
03105: 2. No mundo, ative o pacote de comportamento.
03106: 3. Recarregue o mundo — os scripts rodam no load.
03107: 
03108: Este gerador usa só módulos estáveis, então
03109: NÃO precisa ligar "Beta APIs".
03110: Use /reload no mundo para recarregar os scripts
03111: depois de editar o main.js.`;
03112: 
03113:   const view = document.getElementById('codeView');
03114:   if (tab==='js') view.innerHTML = hl(currentJS,false);
03115:   else if (tab==='mf') view.innerHTML = hl(currentMF,true);
03116:   else view.textContent = tree;

03119:   if (fab) fab.textContent = tab==='mf' ? 'copiar manifest' : 'copiar main.js';

03122:   const total = rules.reduce((s,r)=>s+countActs(r.acts),0);

05855:     pickList.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
05856:       if(!pickTouch || pickTouch.id!==e.pointerId) return;
05857:       if(Math.hypot(e.clientX-pickTouch.x,e.clientY-pickTouch.y)>18) pickTouch.moved=true;
05858:     },{passive:true});
05859:     pickList.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
05860:       if(!pickTouch || pickTouch.id!==e.pointerId) return;
05861:       const p=pickTouch; pickTouch=null;
05862:       const item=e.target.closest('.selpick-item') || p.item;
05863:       if(p.moved || !item || item.disabled) return;
05864:       e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
05865:       chooseOption(item.dataset.value);
05866:     });
05867:     pickList.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{ if(pickTouch?.id===e.pointerId) pickTouch=null; });
05868:   }
05869:   function pickerEls(){
05870:     const ov=document.getElementById('selpick');
05871:     return ov?{ov,panel:ov.querySelector('.selpick-panel'),title:ov.querySelector('.selpick-title'),sub:ov.querySelector('.selpick-sub'),searchWrap:ov.querySelector('.selpick-searchwrap'),search:ov.querySelector('.selpick-search'),list:ov.querySelector('.selpick-list')}:null;
05872:   }
05873:   function guessTitle(sel){
05874:     const direct=sel.parentElement?.querySelector(':scope > label.f') || sel.closest('.params > div')?.querySelector(':scope > label.f');
05875:     if(direct) return direct.textContent.trim();
05876:     if(sel.closest('.card-top')){
05877:       const tag=sel.closest('.step')?.querySelector('.step-tag')?.textContent?.trim();
05878:       if(tag==='QUANDO') return 'Escolher evento';
05879:       if(tag==='SE') return 'Escolher condição';
05880:       if(tag==='ENTÃO') return 'Escolher ação';
05881:     }
05882:     const own=sel.getAttribute('data-picker-title') || sel.getAttribute('aria-label');
05883:     return own || 'Escolher opção';
05884:   }
05885:   function collectOptions(sel){
05886:     const out=[];
05887:     for(const node of Array.from(sel.children)){
05888:       if(node.tagName==='OPTGROUP'){
05889:         out.push({kind:'group',label:node.label||''});
05890:         for(const op of Array.from(node.children)){ if(op.hidden) continue; out.push({kind:'option',group:node.label||'',value:op.value,text:(op.textContent||'').trim(),disabled:op.disabled,selected:op.selected}); }
05891:       }else if(node.tagName==='OPTION'){
05892:         if(!node.hidden) out.push({kind:'option',group:'',value:node.value,text:(node.textContent||'').trim(),disabled:node.disabled,selected:node.selected});
05893:       }
05894:     }
05895:     return out;
05896:   }
05897:   function renderPicker(filter=''){
05898:     const ui=pickerEls(); if(!ui) return;
05899:     const q=filter.trim().toLowerCase();
05900:     const opts=currentEntries.filter(item=>item.kind==='option' && (!q || item.text.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (item.group||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
05901:     if(!opts.length){ ui.list.innerHTML=`<div class="selpick-empty">Nenhuma opção encontrada.</div>`; return; }
05902:     const counts={}; opts.forEach(o=>{ if(o.group) counts[o.group]=(counts[o.group]||0)+1; });
05903:     let html='', lastGroup=null;
05904:     for(const item of opts){
05905:       if(item.group!==lastGroup){
05906:         if(item.group) html += `<div class="selpick-group"><span>${escapeHtml(item.group)}</span><small>${counts[item.group]||0}</small></div>`;
05907:         lastGroup=item.group;
05908:       }
05909:       html += `<button type="button" class="selpick-item ${item.selected?'on':''} ${item.disabled?'disabled':''}" data-value="${escapeAttr(item.value)}" ${item.disabled?'disabled':''}><span class="selpick-check"></span><span class="selpick-item-label">${escapeHtml(item.text)}</span>${item.selected?'<span class="selpick-current">ATUAL</span>':'<span></span>'}</button>`;
05910:     }
05911:     ui.list.innerHTML=html;
05912:     // A seleção é tratada somente por pointerup. Isso evita o segundo click
05913:     // sintético do Android atingir outro seletor depois que o painel fecha.
05914:     ui.list.querySelectorAll('.selpick-item').forEach(btn=>{
05915:       btn.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
05916:         if(e.key==='Enter' || e.key===' '){
05917:           e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); chooseOption(btn.dataset.value);
05918:         }
05919:       });
